FROM appointments AS a

    FROM patients AS p
    WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
) AS p0 ON a.patient_id = p0.patient_id
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p1.practitioner_id, p1.created_at, p1.created_by, p1.first_name, p1.is_active, p1.is_care_navigator, p1.is_deleted, p1.is_supporting_clinician, p1.last_name, p1.npi_number, p1.position, p1.prc_license_number, p1.tenant_id, p1.updated_at, p1.updated_by, p1.user_id

    WHERE p1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
) AS p2 ON a.practitioner_id = p2.practitioner_id

    FROM esas_assessments AS e0

    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1

    SELECT CASE a.status
        WHEN 0 THEN 'Scheduled'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'InProgress'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'Completed'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'Cancelled'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'NoShow'
        ELSE a.status::text
    END
    FROM appointments AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.status <> 3
    ORDER BY a.scheduled_start DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'No Visit')
FROM patients AS p

    FROM esas_assessments AS e0

    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((

        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'

        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'

    FROM advance_directives AS a

    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC

        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'

    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (

    FROM esas_assessments AS e1

SELECT p.patient_id, p.mrn, p.first_name, p.last_name, p.dob, p.created_at, COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.status
        WHEN 0 THEN 'Scheduled'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'InProgress'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'Completed'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'Cancelled'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'NoShow'
        ELSE a.status::text

SELECT p.patient_id, p.mrn, p.first_name, p.last_name, p.dob, p.created_at, COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.status
        WHEN 0 THEN 'Scheduled'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'InProgress'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'Completed'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'Cancelled'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'NoShow'
        ELSE a.status::text
    END
    FROM appointments AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.status <> 3
    ORDER BY a.scheduled_start DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'No Visit')
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name, p.first_name, p.patient_id
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

SELECT p4."LicensureId", p4."LicenseNumber", p4."State", p4."ExpiryDate", p.practitioner_id
FROM practitioners AS p
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT p1.licensure_id AS "LicensureId", p1.license_number AS "LicenseNumber", p1.state AS "State", p1.expiry_date AS "ExpiryDate", p1.practitioner_id
    FROM practitioner_licensures AS p1
    WHERE p1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
) AS p4 ON p.practitioner_id = p4.practitioner_id
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.practitioner_id

FROM practitioners AS p
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT p3.service_area_id AS "ServiceAreaId", p3.zip_code AS "ZipCode", p3.county AS "County", p3.practitioner_id

    WHERE p3.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
) AS p5 ON p.practitioner_id = p5.practitioner_id
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.practitioner_id

SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

2026-05-10 11:41:00.927 +08:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (30ms) [Parameters=[@ef_filter__CurrentTenantId='a0a0a0a0-a0a0-a0a0-a0a0-a0a0a0a0a0a0', @p1='50', @p='0'], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p

SELECT p.patient_id AS "PatientId", p.mrn AS "Mrn", p.first_name AS "FirstName", p.last_name AS "LastName", COALESCE((
    SELECT e.pain
    FROM esas_assessments AS e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e.patient_id
    ORDER BY e.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestPainScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT e0.wellbeing
    FROM esas_assessments AS e0
    WHERE e0.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e0.patient_id
    ORDER BY e0.assessed_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS "LatestWellbeingScore", COALESCE((
    SELECT CASE a.type
        WHEN 0 THEN 'DNR'
        WHEN 1 THEN 'DNI'
        WHEN 2 THEN 'FullCode'
        WHEN 3 THEN 'LivingWill'
        WHEN 4 THEN 'HealthcareProxy'
        WHEN 5 THEN 'ComfortMeasuresOnly'
        ELSE a.type::text
    END
    FROM advance_directives AS a
    WHERE a.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = a.patient_id AND a.is_active
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1), 'None') AS "AdvanceDirectiveType", EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM esas_assessments AS e1
    WHERE e1.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId AND p.patient_id = e1.patient_id AND (e1.pain > 7 OR e1.wellbeing > 7)) AS "IsAlert"
FROM patients AS p
WHERE p.tenant_id = @ef_filter__CurrentTenantId
ORDER BY p.last_name
LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p
